# Complete visual rebuild: cozy adult anime cookbook

Copy this entire document into ChatGPT with image-generation and file/ZIP capabilities. All brief entries are intentional deliverables. The website is an Arabic RTL illustrated cookbook with 35 recipes and 288 illustrated preparation steps.

## Your assignment

Generate every asset listed below as its OWN high-quality PNG image. Create the actual images, save each at its exact relative path, validate them, then return a downloadable ZIP containing the PNGs, README.md and asset-manifest.json. Written descriptions alone do not fulfill this task. Do not return a contact sheet, sprite sheet, multi-panel page, PDF, screenshot of a grid, or one composite containing several deliverables. Do not generate a composite and crop it into pretend independent assets. Each entry requires a separate image generation. Never rename a JPEG/WebP to PNG; decode and export genuine PNG. Do not substitute existing website images.

This is a large production job. If tool or turn limits prevent completing it in one session, work in numbered batches, preserve actual completed files and continue from the manifest. State the precise completed/remaining counts. Never claim an asset or ZIP exists without creating it. Return real partial batch ZIPs when continuation is necessary; the final ZIP must consolidate every accepted asset. Do not silently omit files, send placeholder images, or invent download links. If ZIP/file tools are unavailable, explicitly state this limitation rather than promising an attachment. File capability limits do not justify substituting a collage.

## Locked art direction

A cozy slice-of-life anime cookbook with softly painted shading, delicate confident linework, subtle watercolor/pencil texture and warm natural window light. Food should look delicious and recognizable, with tactile handmade ceramics, linen and wood. Adult anime beauty, expressive warm eyes, calm domestic comfort. Avoid the previous semi-realistic portrait look: clearly anime faces and drawn features, with carefully illustrated food. No glossy 3D, plastic surfaces, photorealism, generic stock-art look, heavy blur, hard flash or harsh saturated colors. Backgrounds are warmly colored and restrained; never pure white except transparent cutouts. Keep enough tonal separation that the subject remains legible on the warm website.

Palette anchors: toasted oat #DFC9AD; warm recipe-card parchment #E9D7BC; caramel #D5B994; terracotta #AA6546; deep forest #314B3C; sage #BEC5A5; honey #E5B17B; espresso #352F25. Treat as visual anchors, not flat-color restrictions.

## Canonical chef: the SAME adult woman in the entire collection

An unmistakably adult 25-year-old anime woman, beautiful and cute, warm brown eyes, chestnut loosely waved hair gathered into a low loose ponytail, olive ribbon, a few soft face-framing strands, gentle blush and welcoming smile. Cream blouse with rolled sleeves, terracotta bib apron, muted olive skirt, simple brown flat shoes when visible. Natural adult proportions, normal hands, believable movement and a modest practical cooking outfit. No childlike face/body, school uniform, revealing outfit or sexualized framing. Her charm comes from expressive anime features and warmth.

Generate character/chef-front.png FIRST and establish it as the canonical reference. Use the actual approved reference image in later image calls, not just this text. Then create the other three reference images individually. Maintain exact face, hair length/color, ribbon, outfit and proportions throughout. Use a single face, no duplicates, no split-screen expressions. If the operator requests a character correction, update the reference before generating the full collection. Do not repeatedly request approvals for every image.

## Kitchen and continuity bible

The same small home kitchen: muted sage cupboards, a honey-caramel wooden worktop, a softly lit window to camera-left, restrained earthy crockery, olive linen, a clean sink and unbranded appliances. Food covers use a close three-quarter view. Action steps may use a tighter overhead/three-quarter camera when necessary for clarity. Keep light from the same side. Prefer the same ceramic bowl, wooden spoon, glass jars, cutting board, cookware and blouse sleeves across each recipe. Match ingredients and physical state exactly from step to step. Do not show finished food before preparation. A cover shows ONE serving; the quantity columns in the website are not invitations to draw three or four meals in every image.

Arabic recipe names, ingredient inventories and step instructions below are the authoritative content. Understand them accurately. Units are grams unless explicitly described otherwise. Ingredient names and quantities guide appearance and proportions; the quantities are NOT text to print in the artwork. Do not invent a garnish, topping, milk, egg, cheese, sauce, fruit or side dish. Do not illustrate an ingredient simply because it is common in a similarly named recipe. Respect raw/cooked distinctions and avoid cross-contamination. Hands must grip tools correctly, cutting boards must be stable, and containers must be plausible for the action. Never depict a person tasting raw meat. Temperature/time instructions remain in HTML outside the image: show the relevant appliance or tool without misleading readable digits.

## Technical delivery contract

- Every listed deliverable is a separate PNG, 8-bit sRGB, high visual quality, no text/logo/watermark/border. PNG is lossless. Use the stated target dimensions/aspect ratio; if the generator cannot output that exact resolution, use its highest native matching aspect ratio and record ACTUAL dimensions. Do not claim native detail from simple upscaling. Do not stretch images.
- Opaque artwork: consistent warm-colored full canvas. Transparent assets: true alpha, clean edges, no baked checkerboard or matte, no clipping of character or props. Only entries explicitly marked transparent use alpha.
- Keep important food, hands and face within the central 85% for safe responsive cropping. Site banners additionally follow their stated text-safe area. Do not put readable words, recipe numbers, labels or UI controls in artwork.
- All paths are relative to the ZIP root, lowercase ASCII. Preserve recipe IDs; their gaps are intentional. Preserve zero-padded step numbers.
- No external image links as substitutes. ZIP must include actual PNG binaries. Do not include copyrighted franchise characters, brand marks, or imitations of a named living artist.

## Generation order and batching

1. Four separate character references.
2. Eight site illustrations, six seamless textures and five isolated decorative props.
3. Each recipe: its cover FIRST, followed by each step in sequence. Pass the actual canonical chef image plus recipe cover/previous step as reference where supported. Never create several steps as panels in one image.
4. Ten kitchen guide illustrations.
5. Validate all files and package the archive.

A sensible checkpoint is one recipe per batch. Preserve the approved artwork across continuations; do not regenerate completed files unnecessarily. Use stable references across batches and keep a working manifest with pending/complete/needs-review status.

### 024. شوفان ليلي • الموز والفانيلا — recipe cover

- **Output:** `recipes/08/cover.png`
- **Target:** 2048 × 2048 px; opaque RGB.
- **Reference:** character/chef-front.png.
- **Image brief:** Recipe: شوفان ليلي • الموز والفانيلا. Exact ingredient inventory for ONE serving: رقائق شوفان: 50 g; زبادي سادة قليل الدسم: 200 g; ماء شرب: 50 g; موز مقشّر: 100 g; خلاصة فانيلا: 1 g. Finished-dish workflow reference: قشّر الموزة. → اهرس نصف الموز بالشوكة. → قطّع النصف الآخر إلى حلقات. → ضع الشوفان والزبادي والماء والفانيلا في مرطبان نظيف سعة 500 مل. → حرّك حتى يبتلّ الشوفان كله. → أضف الفاكهة وقلّب برفق. → أغلق الغطاء بإحكام. → برّد 8 ساعات على الأقل عند 4°م أو أقل. → قلّب الشوفان صباحًا. → إذا ثقل القوام، أضف ملعقة ماء. تناوله باردًا.. Show the same adult chef proudly presenting ONE completed serving, with the final form determined by these steps. The dish occupies the foreground and at least 45% of the composition; her face appears above it. No extra garnishes, drinks, sauces or side dishes not listed. Choose practical cookware for the workflow and lock its appearance for every step in this recipe. Ensure similar recipes are visibly distinguished by their actual ingredients.

### 231. غموس الجزر والفاصوليا البيضاء — recipe cover

- **Output:** `recipes/36/cover.png`
- **Target:** 2048 × 2048 px; opaque RGB.

Exact ingredient inventory for ONE serving: فاصوليا بيضاء مصفّاة: 180 g; جزر: 100 g; خبز تورتيلا: 65 g; زيت زيتون: 5 g; عصير ليمون: 12 g; خيار: 80 g. Finished-dish workflow reference: ابشر الجزر في وعاء للميكروويف. → أضف ملعقة ماء وغطِّ بخفّة. → سخّن 2–3 دقائق حتى يلين. → اشطف الفاصوليا وصفّها. → أضفها إلى الجزر وسخّن دقيقة. → اهرس مع الليمون والزيت. خفّف بالماء عند الحاجة. → حمّص الرغيف في مقلاة جافة، دقيقة لكل جهة. → قطّع الخبز شرائط. → قطّع الخيار أصابع وقدّمه مع الخبز والغموس.. Show the same adult chef proudly presenting ONE completed serving, with the final form determined by these steps. The dish occupies the foreground and at least 45% of the composition; her face appears above it. No extra garnishes, drinks, sauces or side dishes not listed. Choose practical cookware for the workflow and lock its appearance for every step in this recipe. Ensure similar recipes are visibly distinguished by their actual ingredients.

## Required archive and README

Name the final archive `cozy-anime-cookbook-visuals.zip`. It must contain **356 separately generated PNGs**, plus `README.md` and `asset-manifest.json`, directly under the following folders (no absolute filesystem paths):

```text
README.md
asset-manifest.json
character/       4 PNGs
site/            8 PNGs
textures/        6 PNGs
decor/           5 PNGs
recipes/        35 recipe folders, each with cover.png and its numbered steps
                 35 covers + 288 steps = 323 PNGs
guides/         10 PNGs
```

The manifest must contain one record for EACH of the 356 assets with: `file`, `title`, `recipe_id` and `step_number` when applicable, `width`, `height`, `mode`, `transparent`, `status`, `reference_files`, and a concise `notes` field. Use null for inapplicable recipe fields. Actual image dimensions and mode must come from inspecting the output files. Status must distinguish completed from pending/failed files.

Write `README.md` inside the ZIP with these sections:

1. **Collection overview:** visual direction, canonical adult character description, actual completed PNG count, completeness status, generation date and any limitations.
2. **Folder inventory:** exact paths and counts, with the 35 recipe IDs and their step counts (use the recipe mapping below).
3. **Character/style continuity:** which reference to reuse and the locked palette, costume, kitchen and lighting.
4. **Website integration:** `site/home-hero.png` replaces the homepage illustration; chapter files map breakfast/work-lunch/evening-break/before-workout/dinner in order; `site/kitchen-wide.png` is the kitchen banner; `site/footer-chef.png` is a transparent footer option. `recipes/ID/cover.png` replaces that recipe’s `hero` in `source/book.json`; `step-NN.png` replaces its `steps[NN-1].img`. `guides/guide-NN.png` replaces `guides[NN-1].img`. Preserve all recipe text and quantities. The site’s existing relative image paths can be updated after copying this package to `assets/visuals/`, then run `python3 scripts/build.py` and `python3 scripts/check.py`. Do not replace site code in this image-only assignment.
5. **Texture use:** oat-paper is the repeating body background, sage-paper for prep notes, clay-paper for recipe panels, forest-paper for dark welcome/footer areas, olive-linen and warm-wood are optional decorative backgrounds. Textures must tile, remain low contrast, and preserve text readability. Use a small repeat scale such as 512 CSS pixels and adjust after visual inspection. Do not stretch a texture over the whole page. If visible seams remain, mark the asset needs-review.
6. **Decor use:** optional small accents outside ingredient/step content. Never imply an unlisted herb is an ingredient. Transparent chef and props must retain alpha.
7. **Dimensions/transparency:** actual resolution range, RGB/RGBA handling, which files contain alpha, any native-generation limits or resizing performed. High-resolution originals should be retained; optimized WebP/AVIF can be derived later for website performance without discarding the PNG masters.
8. **QA report and missing items:** counts, duplicates, dimensions, alpha, legibility, character consistency, action fidelity and seamless-texture verification. Explicitly list any incomplete or questionable files. Do not label an incomplete archive complete.

## Final quality checks before returning files

- Open and inspect each generated file: not just existence. Verify correct subject, recipe ingredients, step action, character identity, natural hands, safe crop margins and no accidental text.
- Check that all 356 expected PNG paths exist and decode successfully. Confirm true PNG format rather than just filename extension. No zero-byte files, identical copies standing in for different actions, placeholders, collages or missing steps.
- Texture images need four-edge seamless tiling checks with a 2×2 repeat preview. That preview is QA-only, not a replacement for the individual texture.
- Confirm every transparent asset contains genuine alpha and no checkerboard pixels. All other art should have warm opaque backgrounds.
- Confirm counts against manifest and recipe map; reopen the final ZIP and inspect the inventory.
- Return a real downloadable ZIP link plus actual totals and honest limitations. If batching is necessary, return available files and exact remaining paths, and continue when possible.
